<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一潮</rb><rt>ih-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>聚拢来</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>吃饭</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自家人</rb><rt>zi-kô-nying</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>听闻仔</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拉住</rb><rt>l̆a-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大糊的了</rb><rt>do-vu-tih-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>别西卜</rb><rt>Bih-si-poh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>附的个</rb><rt>vu-tih-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>鬼王</rb><rt>kwe-wông</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>拢来</rb><rt>long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，「<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>一国</rb><rt>ih-koh</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>分裂开</rb><rt>feng-lih-kʽæ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一国</rb><rt>ih-koh</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>立弗牢</rb><rt>lih-feh-lao</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>分裂开</rb><rt>feng-lih-kʽæ</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一家</rb><rt>ih-kô</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>立弗牢</rb><rt>lih-feh-lao</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>分裂开</rb><rt>feng-lih-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也是</rb><rt>yia-z</rt><rb>立弗牢</rb><rt>lih-feh-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>M-nying</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>好佬个</rb><rt>hao-lao-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抢夺</rb><rt>tsʽiang-deh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>缚住</rb><rt>bo-djü</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>好佬</rb><rt>hao-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>抢夺</rb><rt>tsʽiang-deh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屋里个</rb><rt>oh-li-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>*tong-si*</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>罪恶</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>亵渎</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>亵渎</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>赦弗掉个</rb><rt>sô-feh-diao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>永远个</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>附的个</rb><rt>vu-tih-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, joh-nying. 或话，熟人。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *Ziu-z*, kwe-wông. 就是鬼王。（原注）</sup>

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一潮</rb><rt>ih-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>坐东</rb><rt>zo-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>身边</rb><rt>sing-pin</rt><rb>坐东个</rb><rt>zo-tong-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>！<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Mk. IV.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走拢</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>*tsih*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>和潮</rb><rt>ʽo-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海涂上</rb><rt>hæ-du-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>教训个</rb><rt>kao-hyüing-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

<sup>3</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>扎开去</rb><rt>tsah-kʽæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鸟</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>飞来</rb><rt>fi-læ</rt><rb>吃掉了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>Yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>烂泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>烂泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>深</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>随手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>抽出来</rb><rt>tsʽiu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>；<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>日头</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>晒瘪</rb><rt>sa-pih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>根头</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>枯落了</rb><rt>kwʽu-lôh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>Yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>缝里</rb><rt>vong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>兴起来</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi-læ</rt><rb>遮煞</rb><rt>tsô-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>呒陶成</rb><rt>m-dao-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>Yiu-sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>泥浴里</rb><rt>nyi-nyüoh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爆出</rb><rt>pao-cʽih</rt><rb>芽头</rb><rt>ngô-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>芽头</rb><rt>*ngô-den*</rt><rb>兴起来</rb><rt>hying-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>陶成</rb><rt>dao-dzing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>来间个</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神国个</rb><rt>Jing-koh-go</rt><rb>秘密</rb><rt>pi-mih</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>dziu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看来</rb><rt>kʽen-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见其</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听闻其</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尚恐怕</rb><rt>zông-kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>罪个</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>饶赦</rb><rt>nyiao-sô</rt></ruby>。』 」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>扎</rb><rt>Tsah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>*iang-ts*</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>*tsah*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>扎间个</rb><rt>tsah-kæn-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>扎的个</rb><rt>tsah-tih-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>夺勒去了</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>欢欢喜喜</rb><rt>hwun-hwun-hyi-hyi</rt><rb>接受个</rb><rt>tsih-ziu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>根头</rb><rt>keng-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>暂时个</rb><rt>dzæn-z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>委曲</rb><rt>w̆e-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>逼难个</rb><rt>pih-næn-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>扎</rb><rt>Tsah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>刺棚</rb><rt>tsʽ-bang</rt><rb>缝里</rb><rt>vong-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>今世个</rb><rt>kying-si-go</rt><rb>愁闷</rb><rt>zeo-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>财物个</rb><rt>dzæ-veh-go</rt><rb>哄骗</rb><rt>hong-pʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>余多个</rb><rt>yü-to-go</rt><rb>私欲</rb><rt>s-yüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>遮煞</rb><rt>tsô-sah</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>呒陶成了</rb><rt>m-dao-dzing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>扎</rb><rt>Tsah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>泥浴里</rb><rt>nyi-nyüoh-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>陶成</rb><rt>dao-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>*kyih*</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>倍</rb><rt>*be*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>*Nying*</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>灯盏</rb><rt>teng-tsæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>斗桶下</rb><rt>teo-dong-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>眠床下</rb><rt>min-zông-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>灯台上</rb><rt>teng-dæ-zông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>S-ʽô</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>显出来</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>隐瞒</rb><rt>ing-mun</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>露出来</rb><rt>lu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听个</rb><rt>tʽing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>量</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>*nying-kô*</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>量</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>听个</rb><rt>tʽing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>加凑</rb><rt>kô-tsʽeo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m̆-teh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>夺勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恶像</rb><rt>ôh-ziang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>日日</rb><rt>nyih-nyih</rt><rb>夜夜</rb><rt>yia-yia</rt><rb>困到</rb><rt>kwʽeng-tao</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>抽苗</rb><rt>tsʽiu-miao</rt><rb>大起来个</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>抽苗</rb><rt>tsʽiu-miao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>结佩</rb><rt>kyih-be</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>佩</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>呼粻</rb><rt>hwu-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>粻</rb><rt>Tsiang</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>足了</rb><rt>tsoh-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>镰刀</rb><rt>lin-tao</rt><rb>动手</rb><rt>dong-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>收割个</rb><rt>siu-keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Dao joh-de. 或话，稻熟了。（原注）</sup>

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>？<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>可比像</rb><rt>Kʽo-pi-ziang</rt><rb>一粒</rb><rt>ih-lih</rt><rb>芥菜子</rb><rt>ka-tsʽæ-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扎</rb><rt>tsah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天凡百</rb><rt>tʽin-væn-pah</rt><rb>秧子</rb><rt>iang-ts</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>小个</rb><rt>siao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>扎落仔</rb><rt>tsah-lôh-ts</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>发出来</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>成勒</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>菜蔬</rb><rt>tsʽæ-su</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>桠枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>鸟</rb><rt>tiao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉勒</rb><rt>deng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>遮荫</rb><rt>tsô-ing</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>KEH</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>渡过</rb><rt>du-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇岸</rb><rt>keh-ngen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>散开了</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ-leh</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是介</rb><rt>ziu-z-ka</rt><rb>船里</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>带去</rb><rt>ta-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>别只</rb><rt>bih-tsah</rt><rb>小船</rb><rt>siao-jün</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>Do-do</rt><rb>风水</rb><rt>fong-shü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>发作</rb><rt>fah-tsôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>浪</rb><rt>lông</rt><rb>拍进</rb><rt>pʽah-tsing</rt><rb>船里</rb><rt>jün-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>满了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>后梢</rb><rt>ʽeo-sao</rt><rb>垫勒</rb><rt>din-leh</rt><rb>枕头</rb><rt>tsing-deo</rt><rb>困熟的</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>调觉</rb><rt>diao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>倒没</rb><rt>tao-meh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>管账</rb><rt>kwun-tsiang</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>责罚</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>静带落</rb><rt>Zing-tæn-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>风</rb><rt>Fong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>歇了</rb><rt>hyih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>太太平平了</rb><rt>tʽa-tʽa-bing-bing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>胆子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>？」<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>讲来讲去</rb><rt>kông-læ-kông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依顺</rb><rt>i-jing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>！」

---

### Mk. V.

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>对岸</rb><rt>te-ngen</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>加大拉</rb><rt>Kyüô-da-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>坟窠里</rb><rt>veng-kʽo-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走出来个</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>附的</rb><rt>vu-tih</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>坟窠里</rb><rt>veng-kʽo-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>链条</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>锁勒</rb><rt>so-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>镣铐</rb><rt>liao-kʽao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>链条</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt><rb>屡次</rb><rt>le-tsʽ</rt><rb>锁勒</rb><rt>so-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>链条</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>折断</rb><rt>cih-dön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>镣铐</rb><rt>liao-kʽao</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弄掉</rb><rt>long-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>制服</rb><rt>cü-voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>日日</rb><rt>Nyih-nyih</rt><rb>夜夜</rb><rt>yia-yia</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>坟窠里</rb><rt>veng-kʽo-li</rt><rb>㗇㗇嘶</rb><rt>yüô-yüô-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>打伤</rb><rt>tang-sông</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>远远</rb><rt>Yün-yün</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>奔拢来</rb><rt>peng-long-læ</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>怀怀</rb><rt>wæ-wæ</rt><rb>讴响</rb><rt>eo-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>顶</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>相干</rb><rt>siang-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>求恳</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>磨难</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> （<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」）<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>营</rb><rt>Ying</rt></ruby>』，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*Kyü*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>再三</rb><rt>tsæ-sæn</rt><rb>求恳</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>食</rb><rt>zih</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>求恳</rb><rt>gyiu-kʽeng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>队里</rb><rt>de-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>窜落</rb><rt>tsʽön-lôh</rt><rb>山岩</rb><rt>sæn-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>投勒</rb><rt>deo-leh</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>约来</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二千</rb><rt>nyi-tsʽin</rt><rb>只数</rb><rt>tsah-su</rt></ruby>）<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>溢煞了</rb><rt>ih-sah-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>Kʽen</rt><rb>泥猪</rb><rt>nyi-cü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>奔起</rb><rt>peng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>乡下</rb><rt>hyiang-ʽô</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>入魔</rb><rt>jih-mo</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>早已</rb><rt>tsao-yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一营</rb><rt>ih-ying</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*kyü*</rt><rb>来的个</rb><rt>læ-tih-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐东</rb><rt>zo-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>穿的</rb><rt>cʽün-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>清通了</rb><rt>tsʽing-tʽong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怕起来了</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇班</rb><rt>Keh-pæn</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>入魔个</rb><rt>jih-mo-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>泥猪个</rb><rt>nyi-cü-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>入魔个</rb><rt>jih-mo-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>做队</rb><rt>tso-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反向</rb><rt>fæn-hyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>跑</rb><rt>Bao</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自家人</rb><rt>zi-kô-nying</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>可怜</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>地加波利</rb><rt>Di-kyüô-po-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>传开</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>渡过</rb><rt>du-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>对岸</rb><rt>te-ngen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>管</rb><rt>Kwun</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>睚鲁</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>再三</rb><rt>tsæ-sæn</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>临终了</rb><rt>ling-cong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>*tsʽing*</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一按</rb><rt>ih-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活了</rb><rt>weh-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拥勒</rb><rt>üong-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生勒</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>血漏</rb><rt>hyüih-leo</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>医生</rb><rt>i-sang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>费掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>瘥点</rb><rt>tsʽô-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>厉害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>后背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>摸摸</rb><rt>moh-moh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>*sing*</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>*li-hyiang*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>只消</rb><rt>tsih-siao</rt><rb>摸摸</rb><rt>moh-moh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>血漏个</rb><rt>hyüih-leo-go</rt><rb>病源</rb><rt>bing-nyün</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>止住</rb><rt>ts-djü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>医好了</rb><rt>i-hao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>扭带转</rb><rt>nyin-tæn-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>摸</rb><rt>moh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>？」<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>挨着</rb><rt>a-djôh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>摸</rb><rt>moh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>吓勒</rb><rt>hah-leh</rt><rb>刮刮抖</rb><rt>gwah-gwah-teo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>做过个</rb><rt>tso-ko-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>老实</rb><rt>lao-jih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>囡</rb><rt>Nön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安安稳稳</rb><rt>en-en-w̆eng-w̆eng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>痊愈</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>Wa</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>*oh-li*</rt><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何用</rb><rt>ʽo-yüong</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt><rb>着力</rb><rt>dziah-lih</rt></ruby>？」<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>Hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>Tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>闹热彭生</rb><rt>nao-nyih-bang-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>号泪大哭</rb><rt>ʽao-li-da-kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>闹热彭生</rb><rt>nao-nyih-bang-sang</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>娘子</rb><rt>Nyiang-ts</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>困熟的</rb><rt>kwʽeng-joh-tih</rt></ruby>。」<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>冷笑</rb><rt>lang-siao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>娘子个</rb><rt>nyiang-ts-go</rt><rb>爹娘</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt><rb>困个</rb><rt>kwʽeng-go</rt><rb>房里</rb><rt>vông-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>大利大</rb><rt>Da-li-da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>古米</rb><rt>kwu-mi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>翻出来</rb><rt>fæn-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>娘子</rb><rt>Nyiang-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>娘子</rb><rt>Nyiang-ts</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走了</rb><rt>tseo-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>岁</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弄大</rb><rt>long-do</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>再三</rb><rt>tsæ-sæn</rt><rb>叮嘱</rb><rt>ting-côh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>箇起</rb><rt>keh-kyʽi</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>挔点</rb><rt>i-tin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt><rb>吃吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。

### Mk. VI.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>本</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聚会堂里</rb><rt>jü-we-dông-li</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>聪明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>亲手</rb><rt>tsʽing-siu</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>！<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>马利亚</u>个</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>木匠</rb><rt>moh-ziang</rt><rb>师父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>*Kyʽi*</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>*feh-z*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>约西</rb><rt>Iah-si</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>西门</u>个</rb><rt>Si-meng-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>Hyüong-di</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>住东</rb><rt>djü-tong</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>厌憎</rb><rt>in-tseng</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先知人</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>亲眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>呒体面个</rb><rt>m-tʽi-min-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>病人</rb><rt>bing-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>走遍</rb><rt>tseo-pin</rt><rb>团圈个</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>两两</rb><rt>liang-liang</rt><rb>搭班</rb><rt>tah-pæn</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>*hao*</rt><rb>制服</rb><rt>*cü-voh*</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>棒</rb><rt>bông</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>用个</rb><rt>yüong-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>包袱</rb><rt>pao-voh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>缭缴里个</rb><rt>liao-kyiao-li-go</rt><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>鞋</rb><rt>ʽa</rt><rb>穿仔</rb><rt>cʽün-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>双倍</rb><rt>shông-be</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>随便</rb><rt>Ze-bin</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>一份</rb><rt>ih-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>甩掉</rb><rt>hwah-diao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>脚底下个</rb><rt>kyiah-ti-ʽô-go</rt><rb>烂泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话向</rb><rt>wô-hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>所多玛</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>蛾摩拉</u>个</rb><rt>Ngo-mo-lah-go</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>ying-vah</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>轻可</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽo</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*Meng-du*</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>*eo*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>回心转意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>傅</rb><rt>fu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>生病</rb><rt>sang-bing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>（<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>名声</rb><rt>ming-sing</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>扬开了</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>），<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>行洗礼个</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来了</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>Yiu-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。」<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>古时节</rb><rt>*kwu-z-tsin*</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>斩</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来个</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>腓力</u>个</rb><rt>Fi-lih-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希罗底</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-lo-ti-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>缚</rb><rt>bo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牢监里</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希罗底</rb><rt>Hyi-lo-ti</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>兄弟个</rb><rt>hyüong-di-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希罗底</rb><rt>Hyi-lo-ti</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>冤恨</rb><rt>ün-ʽeng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做人</rb><rt>tso-nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>保护</rb><rt>pao-wu</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>机会个</rb><rt>kyi-we-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>生日</rb><rt>sang-nyih</rt><rb>办酒</rb><rt>bæn-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>贵重个</rb><rt>kwe-djong-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希罗底</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-lo-ti-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>走进来</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-læ</rt><rb>跳舞</rb><rt>tʽiao-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希律</rb><rt>Hyi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>同席个</rb><rt>dong-zih-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>国王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>随</rb><rt>Ze</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罚</rb><rt>vah</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>咒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>Feh-leng</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>国家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>一半</rb><rt>ih-pun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>娘子</rb><rt>Nyiang-ts</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>Ah-nyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>行洗礼</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>国王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>行洗礼</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>盘里</rb><rt>bun-li</rt><rb>𣥼仔</rb><rt>tsi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>国王</rb><rt>Koh-wông</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>忧闷</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>罚过咒</rb><rt>vah-ko-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>同席个</rb><rt>dong-zih-go</rt><rb>人客</rb><rt>nying-kʽah</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>回复</rb><rt>we-foh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>国王</rb><rt>Koh-wông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>刽子手</rb><rt>kwe-ts-siu</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牢监里</rb><rt>lao-kæn-li</rt><rb>斩</rb><rt>tsæn</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>盘里</rb><rt>bun-li</rt><rb>𣥼仔</rb><rt>tsi-ts</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>娘子</rb><rt>nyiang-ts</rt><rb>驮去</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>使徒</rb><rt>S-DU</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>将息</rb><rt>tsiang-sih</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>来来去去个</rb><rt>læ-læ-kyʽi-kyʽi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>吃饭</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒工夫</rb><rt>m-kong-fu</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>步行奔拢</rb><rt>bu-ʽang-peng-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走到</rb><rt>tseo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>慈悲心</rb><rt>dz-pe-sing</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>成管个</rb><rt>dzing-kwun-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>乡下</rb><rt>hyiang-ʽô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>村上</rb><rt>tsʽeng-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*Meng-du*</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>廿两</rb><rt>nyiæn-liang</rt><rb>银子个</rb><rt>nying-ts-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！」<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看看相</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen-siang</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>看过仔</rb><rt>kʽen-ko-ts</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>五个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>两梗</rb><rt>liang-kwang</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>青草</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽao</rt><rb>滩里</rb><rt>tʽæn-li</rt><rb>总打总</rb><rt>tsong-tang-tsong</rt><rb>坐带落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup>
