<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>两梗</rb><rt>liang-kwang</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝福仔</rb><rt>coh-foh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>𤖼带开</rb><rt>pʽah-tæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>摆</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两梗</rb><rt>liang-kwang</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>Long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>吃过</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吃饱了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>收拾</rb><rt>Siu-jih</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>满满</rb><rt>mun-mun</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>篮</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>收拾</rb><rt>*siu-jih*</rt><rb>吃剩个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-dzing-go</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>约来</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>45</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>随手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>催</rb><rt>tsʽe</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>对岸</rb><rt>te-ngen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯赛大</rb><rt>Pah-sæ-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇大潮</rb><rt>keh-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>别开仔</rb><rt>bih-kʽæ-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>Yi-kying</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇只</rb><rt>keh-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海个</rb><rt>hæ-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>独自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>岸上</rb><rt>ngen-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>撑勒</rb><rt>tsʽang-leh</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>吃力</rb><rt>kyʽih-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>斗风个</rb><rt>teo-fong-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>当夜</rb><rt>tông-yia</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt><rb>四更</rb><rt>s-kang</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>面顶</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走勒去</rb><rt>tseo-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>过头</rb><rt>ko-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>面顶</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>显灵</rb><rt>hyin-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怀怀响</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吃𢞕了</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>一声</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>放心</rb><rt>Fông-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>船里</rb><rt>jün-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>风</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>息了</rb><rt>sih-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>分外</rb><rt>veng-nga</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>忖弗到</rb><rt>tsʽeng-feh-tao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>馒头个</rb><rt>mun-deo-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>渡过</rb><rt>du-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>革尼撒勒</rb><rt>Keh-nyi-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>岸</rb><rt>ngen</rt></ruby>。<sup>54</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>船里</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>奔转</rb><rt>peng-cün</rt><rb>四凸乱圈</rb><rt>s-deh-lön-kyʽün</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>铺板</rb><rt>pʽu-pæn</rt><rb>抬勒</rb><rt>dæ-leh</rt><rb>生病</rb><rt>sang-bing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抬</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>走进个</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>村上</rb><rt>tsʽeng-zông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>乡下</rb><rt>hyiang-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>生病个</rb><rt>sang-bing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>求求</rb><rt>gyiu-gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>衦头</rb><rt>ken-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>按个</rb><rt>en-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好了</rb><rt>hao-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Mk. VII.

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>Kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>洁净<sup> </sup>个</rb><rt>kyih-zing-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>吃饭</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt></ruby>；（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>滰过个</rb><rt>gyiang-ko-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>）<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>批评</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> （<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>传落来个</rb><rt>djün-lôh-læ-go</rt><rb>古典</rb><rt>kwu-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>小小心心</rb><rt>siao-siao-sing-sing</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>吃饭</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>市面上</rb><rt>z-min-zông</rt><rb>归来</rb><rt>*kyü-læ*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>别样告</rb><rt>bih-nyiang-kao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把守个</rb><rt>pô-siu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>碗盏</rb><rt>un-tsæn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>铜盆</rb><rt>dong-beng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>榻上</rb><rt>tʽah-zông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。）<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>问问</rb><rt>meng-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>传落来个</rb><rt>djün-lôh-læ-go</rt><rb>古典</rb><rt>kwu-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>作</rb><rt>tsôh</rt><rb>滰手</rb><rt>gyiang-siu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吃饭</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-væn</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh-wô*, se-djông-go. 或话，随常个。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh-wô*, peh-djông; *ʽôh*, ih-dzih tao siu-tsang-cü-den; *ʽôh*, s gyün-deo gyiang. 或话，不常；或一直到手㬹株头；或，使拳头滰。（原注）</sup>

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>以赛亚</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>假好人</rb><rt>kô-hao-nying</rt><rb>预先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>离远</rb><rt>li-yün</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>当</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>*Jing-ming-go*</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>白力力</rb><rt>bah-lih-lih</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把守</rb><rt>pô-siu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>传落来个</rb><rt>djün-lôh-læ-go</rt><rb>古典</rb><rt>kwu-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>碗盏</rb><rt>un-tsæn</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>废掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>特为</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>把守</rb><rt>pô-siu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>传落来个</rb><rt>djün-lôh-læ-go</rt><rb>古典</rb><rt>kwu-tin</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>父母</rb><rt>vu-meo</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>𧮙骂</rb><rt>Zôh-mô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>应当</rb><rt>ing-tông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>各般</rb><rt>Koh-pæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>，——<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好处</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>传开个</rb><rt>djün-kʽæ-go</rt><rb>古典</rb><rt>kwu-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>废掉</rb><rt>fi-diao</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>走进个</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>出来个</rb><rt>cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>比方个</rb><rt>pi-fông-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>Lin</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>啥</rb><rt>Soh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt></ruby>？<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>行落</rb><rt>*ʽang-lôh*</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>解出</rb><rt>ka-cʽih</rt><rb>坑子里</rb><rt>kʽang-ts-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>化</rb><rt>hwô</rt><rb>干净了</rb><rt>ken-zing-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>出来个</rb><rt>cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>发出来个</rb><rt>fah-cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>恶个</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt><rb>忖头</rb><rt>tsʽeng-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>偷</rb><rt>tʽeo</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嫖</rb><rt>biao</rt><rb>婊子</rb><rt>piao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>凶手</rb><rt>hyüong-siu</rt></ruby>，<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>盗贼</rb><rt>dao-zeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>贪财</rb><rt>tʽen-dzæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恶毒</rb><rt>ôh-doh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奸诈</rb><rt>kæn-tsô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>私欲</rb><rt>s-yüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>妒忌</rb><rt>tu-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>谤毁</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>骄傲</rb><rt>kyiao-ngao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>狂妄</rb><rt>gwông-vông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>坏处</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推罗</rb><rt>Tʽe-lo</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>西顿</rb><rt>Si-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>一份</rb><rt>ih-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>隐瞒弗来</rb><rt>ing-mun-feh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>附的</rb><rt>vu-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走来</rb><rt>tseo-læ</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>脚下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>叙利腓尼基</rb><rt>Jü-li-Fi-nyi-kyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>该</rb><rt>Kæ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>吃饱</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>儿囡个</rb><rt>ng-nô-go</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>黄狗</rb><rt>wun-kyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>黄狗</rb><rt>wun-kyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>桌凳下</rb><rt>coh-teng-ʽô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>跌落个</rb><rt>tih-lôh-go</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>走出了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>走出了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>囡</rb><rt>nön</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>眠床里</rb><rt>min-zông-li</rt><rb>困间</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh-wô*, bih-koh-nying. 或话，别国人。（原注）</sup>

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推罗</rb><rt>Tʽe-lo</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>西顿</rb><rt>Si-teng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>地加波利</rb><rt>Di-kyüô-po-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>转过</rb><rt>cün-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>聋𦗭</rb><rt>long-bang</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>吃舌头</rb><rt>keh-zih-din</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>领勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>指头</rb><rt>ts-deo</rt><rb>弄进</rb><rt>l̆ong-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>耳朵里</rb><rt>ng-to-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吐</rb><rt>tʽu</rt><rb>馋唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>舌头</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt></ruby>，<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>叹</rb><rt>tʽæn</rt><rb>一口</rb><rt>ih-kʽeo</rt><rb>气</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>以法大</rb><rt>Yi-fah-da</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>开开来</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ-læ</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开了</rb><rt>kʽæ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>舌头个</rb><rt>zih-deo-go</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>解散</rb><rt>ka-sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>豪𢜶了</rb><rt>ʽao-sao-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叮嘱</rb><rt>ting-côh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>越</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>叮嘱</rb><rt>ting-côh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>越发</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>扬开</rb><rt>yiang-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>极头</rb><rt>gyih-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做勒</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聋𦗭</rb><rt>long-bang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>哑子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Mk. VIII.

<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>KEH-GO</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>聚拢东</rb><rt>jü-long-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>2</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>哀怜</rb><rt>æ-lin</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>饿勒</rb><rt>ngo-leh</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>路里</rb><rt>lu-li</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>脱力</rb><rt>tʽeh-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>远远</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>荡头</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>何里来</rb><rt>ʽah-li-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃饱</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>七个</rb><rt>Tsʽih-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>坐带落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝谢仔</rb><rt>coh-zia-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𤖼开</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>摆带开</rb><rt>pa-tæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>摆</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几梗</rb><rt>kyi-kwang</rt><rb>小小个</rb><rt>siao-siao-go</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>*yi*</rt><rb>祝福仔</rb><rt>coh-foh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>摆间</rb><rt>pa-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>*Long-tsong*</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>吃过</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>吃饱了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>吃剩个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-dzing-go</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt><rb>收拾拢</rb><rt>siu-jih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>篮</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>约来</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>四千</rb><rt>s-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>随手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>大玛努大</rb><rt>Da-mô-nu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>盘问</rb><rt>bun-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天里个</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>意思</rb><rt>*i-s*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>叹气</rb><rt>tʽæn-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>Tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>兆头</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>对岸</rb><rt>te-ngen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*Meng-du*</rt><rb>忘记</rb><rt>mông-kyi</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>船里</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>别样</rb><rt>bih-yiang</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叮嘱</rb><rt>ting-côh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>小心</rb><rt>siao-sing</rt><rb>顾着</rb><rt>kwu-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>希律</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-lih-go</rt><rb>酵水</rb><rt>kao-shü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*Meng-du*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>啥</rb><rt>Soh</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt></ruby>？<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>𤖼开</rb><rt>pʽah-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>满满</rb><rt>mun-mun</rt><rb>几</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>篮</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>Jih-nyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> 「<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七个</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>*mun-deo*</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>四千</rb><rt>s-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>满满</rb><rt>mun-mun</rt><rb>几</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>篮</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>七</rb><rt>Tsʽih</rt><rb>篮</rb><rt>*læn*</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>*Ka-ni*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>拉</rb><rt>lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>*wa*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>Tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯赛大</rb><rt>Pah-sæ-da</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>摸摸</rb><rt>moh-moh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拉勒</rb><rt>l̆a-leh</rt><rb>瞎子个</rb><rt>hah-ts-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>吐</rb><rt>tʽu</rt><rb>馋唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>眼睛里</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>*Hah-ts*</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>抬带起</rb><rt>dæ-tæn-kyʽi</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>上好样</rb><rt>zông-hao-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>看勒</rb><rt>kʽen-leh</rt><rb>清通了</rb><rt>tsʽing-tʽong-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>乡村里</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng-li</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒利亚</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-li-üô</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>腓力比</rb><rt>Fi-lih-pi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>Nying-kô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有得</rb><rt>*Yiu-teh*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>行洗礼个</rb><rt>ʽAng-si-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>有得</rb><rt>yiu-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>内中</rb><rt>nen-cong</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>叮嘱</rb><rt>ting-côh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>长老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-leh</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>𠡒𠡒</rb><rt>te-te</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拦阻</rb><rt>læn-tsu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>责罚</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>liu-sing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>单</rb><rt>tæn</rt><rb>留心</rb><rt>*liu-sing*</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>应当</rb><rt>ing-tông</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>揹勒</rb><rt>pe-leh</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>丧掉</rb><rt>sông-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>*kʽeng*</rt><rb>丧掉</rb><rt>sông-diao</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>反转</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>保全</rb><rt>pao-djün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>遍天下个</rb><rt>pin-tʽin-ʽô-go</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>*dzæ-veh*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>活灵</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽÔh-tsia</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>取赎</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活灵</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>罪恶个</rb><rt>ze-ôh-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>圣善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *Keh* *liang* *tsih*, *fæn* sing-ming, *fæn* weh-ling, *peng-veng* *tso* *z*. 箇两节，翻性命，翻活灵，本文做字。（原注）</sup>

### Mk. IX.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>荡头</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>立东个</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>KO-LEH</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>高高</rb><rt>kao-kao</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>变出</rb><rt>pin-cʽih</rt><rb>各样</rb><rt>kôh-yiang</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>发出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雪</rb><rt>shih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>白白个</rb><rt>bah-bah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>漂白</rb><rt>pʽiao-bah</rt><rb>师父</rb><rt>s-vu</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>白个</rb><rt>bah-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>现出东</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>三顶</rb><rt>sæn-ting</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一顶</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一顶</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>一顶</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一朵</rb><rt>ih-tô</rt><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>盖住</rb><rt>kæ-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>云</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>亲爱个</rb><rt>tsʽing-æ-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>忽然</rb><rt>hweh-jün</rt><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>走落</rb><rt>Tseo-lôh</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叮嘱</rb><rt>ting-côh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>印的</rb><rt>ing-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>请问</rb><rt>tsʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？」<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自然</rb><rt>z-jün</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挽回</rb><rt>w̆æn-we</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>圣书里</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>苦难</rb><rt>kwʽu-næn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>看弗起</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>来过了</rb><rt>læ-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>候</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>圣书里</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>TSEO</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>围拢间</rb><rt>yü-long-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>和潮</rb><rt>ʽO-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>惊骇</rb><rt>kying-hyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>奔拢来</rb><rt>peng-long-læ</rt><rb>招接</rb><rt>tsiao-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>Keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>哑子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>附的个</rb><rt>vu-tih-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>附着</rb><rt>vu-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>磕倒</rb><rt>kʽeh-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>黏馋</rb><rt>nyin-zæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>牙齿</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>咬勒</rb><rt>ngao-leh</rt><rb>紧紧</rb><rt>kying-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>僵拢起</rb><rt>kyiang-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>哎</rb><rt>ʽE</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>世代</rb><rt>shü-dæ</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>拢来</rb><rt>long-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>一时里</rb><rt>ih-z-li</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>活撞活颠</rb><rt>weh-djông-weh-tin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>翻来覆去</rb><rt>fæn-læ-foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>黏馋</rb><rt>nyin-zæn</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>几时了</rb><rt>kyi-z-de</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>*z-ka-go*</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*Kyü*</rt><rb>不常</rb><rt>peh-djông</rt><rb>丢</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>不常</rb><rt>*peh-djông*</rt><rb>丢</rb><rt>*tiu*</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*gyi*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>水里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>除灭</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>发</rb><rt>fah</rt><rb>慈悲心</rb><rt>dz-pe-sing</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>*peh*</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>百样</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>能够个</rb><rt>neng-keo-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>小人个</rb><rt>Siao-nying-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>眼里</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>帮助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>奔拢来</rb><rt>peng-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>哑子</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>聋𦗭个</rb><rt>long-bang-go</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*Kyü*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怀怀响</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>活撞活颠</rb><rt>weh-djông-weh-tin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走出了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>*siao-nying*</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>甚至于</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>挡</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立起来</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>？」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>Keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>*kyü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>禁食</rb><rt>kying-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走过</rb><rt>tseo-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗敢</rb><rt>feh-ken</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百农</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>路上</rb><rt>lu-zông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴拢</rb><rt>eo-long</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>头个</rb><rt>deo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>众人个</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>结煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>众人个</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>领勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>当中央</rb><rt>tông-cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>抱勒</rb><rt>bao-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>37</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>*tsih-ziu*</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>谤毁</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>对头</rb><rt>te-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>帮手</rb><rt>pông-siu</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>一杯</rb><rt>ih-pe</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>个</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt><rb>报应</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>磨上层</rb><rt>mo-zông-zeng</rt><rb>挂</rb><rt>kwô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>头颈</rb><rt>deo-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>丢落</rb><rt>tiu-lôh</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>刑罚</rb><rt>*ying-vah*</rt><rb>轻可</rb><rt>kyʽing-kʽo</rt></ruby>。

<sup>43</sup> 「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>斩</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>残疾</rb><rt>dzæn-dzih</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>两只</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>地狱</rb><rt>di-nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>熓个</rb><rt>u-go</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>虫</rb><rt>djong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>熓</rb><rt>u</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。 <sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>斩</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>拐脚</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>两只</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>脚</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>丢落</rb><rt>tiu-lôh</rt><rb>地狱</rb><rt>di-nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>熓个</rb><rt>u-go</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>虫</rb><rt>djong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>熓</rb><rt>u</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>带累</rb><rt>ta-li</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>𫦉</rb><rt>l̆eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>能使</rb><rt>neng-s</rt><rb>独眼</rb><rt>doh-ngæn</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>双眼</rb><rt>shông-ngæn</rt><rb>丢落</rb><rt>tiu-lôh</rt><rb>地狱个</rb><rt>di-nyüoh-go</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>虫</rb><rt>djong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>熓</rb><rt>u</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>49</sup> 「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>煅</rb><rt>tön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>盐</rb><rt>yin</rt><rb>盐</rb><rt>yin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。 <sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>盐</rb><rt>Yin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>盐</rb><rt>yin</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt><rb>咸个</rb><rt>ʽæn-go</rt><rb>味道</rb><rt>mi-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>咸</rb><rt>ʽæn</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>盐</rb><rt>yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>和睦</rb><rt>ʽo-moh</rt></ruby>。」
