### Mk. X.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>动身</rb><rt>dong-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河外</rb><rt>ʽo-nga</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>犹太</u>个</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa-go</rt><rb>地界</rb><rt>di-ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>聚拢来</rb><rt>jü-long-læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>闲常</rb><rt>ʽæn-djông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 33. 耶（稣）后（头）33（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>离掉</rb><rt>li-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>作兴</rb><rt>tsôh-hying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>作兴</rb><rt>tsôh-hying</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>意思</rb><rt>*i-s*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>*nen-nyüing*</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>离书</rb><rt>li-shü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>离掉</rb><rt>li-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>心肠</rb><rt>sing-dziang</rt><rb>硬</rb><rt>ngang</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>开辟</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一男</rb><rt>ih-nen</rt><rb>一女</rb><rt>ih-nyü</rt></ruby>。』 <sup>7</sup> 『<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>爹娘</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>亲近</rb><rt>tsʽing-gying</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>成</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>为</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>一体</rb><rt>ih-tʽi</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>是介个</rb><rt>Z-ka-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>一体</rb><rt>ih-tʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>配拢个</rb><rt>pʽe-long-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>离掉</rb><rt>li-diao</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>娶</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>*lao-nyüing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>嫁</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>*nen-nyüing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>*YIU-SING*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>领勒</rb><rt>ling-leh</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>阻住</rb><rt>tsu-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇种介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神国</rb><rt>Jing-koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抱</rb><rt>bao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝福</rb><rt>coh-foh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>IH</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>路街里</rb><rt>lu-ka-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>奔拢来</rb><rt>peng-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跪倒</rb><rt>gyü-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问问</rb><rt>meng-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>？」<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>Dza-we</rt><rb>称呼</rb><rt>tsʽing-hwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>Ih-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗可</rb><rt>feh-kʽo</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>杀</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>偷盗</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>谎告</rb><rt>hwông-kao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>索诈</rb><rt>soh-tsô</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>』『<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>父母</rb><rt>vu-meo</rt></ruby>』。 」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt><rb>顾着个</rb><rt>kwu-djôh-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>缺</rb><rt>kyʽüih</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>通</rb><rt>tʽong</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>穷人</rb><rt>gyüong-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>财物</rb><rt>dzæ-veh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>揹勒</rb><rt>pe-leh</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>失色</rb><rt>shih-seh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>忧闷相</rb><rt>iu-meng-siang</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>团圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>难</rb><rt>næn</rt></ruby>！」<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>小人</rb><rt>Siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>倚侍</rb><rt>i-z</rt><rb>家计</rb><rt>kô-kyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>难</rb><rt>næn</rt></ruby>！<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>骆驼</rb><rt>Loh-do</rt><rb>穿过</rb><rt>cʽün-ko</rt><rb>针眼</rb><rt>tsing-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>容易</rb><rt>yüong-yi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>吃𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>？」<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>能够个</rb><rt>*neng-keo-go*</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>无所不能</rb><rt>vu-sô-peh-neng</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>弃掉仔</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>爹娘</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>老婆</rb><rt>lao-bo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>今世</rb><rt>kying-si</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>倍个</rb><rt>be-go</rt><rb>屋宇</rb><rt>oh-yü</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>田地</rb><rt>din-di</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*keh-sing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>逼难个</rb><rt>pih-næn-go</rt><rb>事体</rb><rt>z-tʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>下世</rb><rt>ʽô-si</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>*feh*</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>*teh-djôh*</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，——<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>呒得个</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>在前个</rb><rt>dzæ-zin-go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>落后</rb><rt>lôh-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>许多</rb><rt>*hyü-to*</rt><rb>在后个</rb><rt>dzæ-ʽeo-go</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>上前</rb><rt>zông-zin</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>ZÔNG</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>惊骇</rb><rt>kying-hyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>怕惧</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>第二遭</rb><rt>di-nyi-tsao</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>碰着</rb><rt>pʽong-djôh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：<sup>33</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>读书人</rb><rt>doh-shü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>死罪</rb><rt>si-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>解去</rb><rt>ka-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别国人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>别国人</rb><rt>*bih-koh-nying*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>糟蹋</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馋唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt><rb>吐</rb><rt>tʽu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西庇太</rb><rt>Si-pi-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>、<u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>走拢来</rb><rt>tseo-long-læ</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但愿</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>？」<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>荣华个</rb><rt>yüong-wô-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>借手边</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>求个</rb><rt>gyiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>受个</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>能够个</rb><rt>Neng-keo-go</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>吃个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>受个</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>受个</rb><rt>ziu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt><rb>借手边</rb><rt>tsia-siu-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>*Ah-tia*</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>预备好间个</rb><rt>yü-be-hao-kæn-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>十个</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>*meng-du*</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怏了</rb><rt>ông-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>别国</rb><rt>Bih-koh</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>压实</rb><rt>ah-jih</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>执掌</rb><rt>tsih-công</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大个</rb><rt>do-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>头个</rb><rt>deo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>所出</rb><rt>sô-cʽih</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>替代</rb><rt>tʽi-dæ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\]*ʽÔh* *wô*, tso wông-ti go. 或话，做皇帝个。（原注）</sup>

<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶利哥</rb><rt>Yiæ-li-ko</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴底买</rb><rt>Pô-Ti-mæ</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>底买</u>个</rb><rt>Ti-mæ-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>坐仔</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>讨饭</rb><rt>tʽao-væn</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>Ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>个</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>可怜</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>许多</rb><rt>Hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>狠</rb><rt>heng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>反转</rb><rt>fæn-cün</rt><rb>分外</rb><rt>veng-nga</rt><rb>讴勒</rb><rt>eo-leh</rt><rb>响</rb><rt>hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽEo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可怜</rb><rt>kʽo-lin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>49</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立落</rb><rt>lih-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>放心</rb><rt>fông-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>Hah-ts</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爬起</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>Hah-ts</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>拉波尼</rb><rt>Lah-po-nyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>。」<sup>53</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。」<ruby><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>*Hah-ts*</rt><rb>马上</rb><rt>mô-zông</rt><rb>看见了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依路</rb><rt>i-lu</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\]*ʽÔh* *wô*, i ng hao. 或话，医尔好。（原注）</sup>
